--- FILE: .agents/rules/venther-mobile.md ---
# Venther.App - Diretrizes de Desenvolvimento Mobile

## 1. Stack TecnolÃ³gica
- **Framework:** .NET MAUI (.NET 10) focado em Android.
- **PadrÃ£o de Arquitetura:** MVVM com `CommunityToolkit.Mvvm`.
- **Toolkit:** `CommunityToolkit.Maui`.
- **NavegaÃ§Ã£o:** .NET MAUI Shell (`AppShell`).

## 2. PadrÃ£o MVVM & InjeÃ§Ã£o de DependÃªncia
- Todas as ViewModels herdam de `BaseViewModel` (`ObservableObject`).
- Uso dos source generators do Toolkit: `[ObservableProperty]` e `[RelayCommand]`.
- ViewModels e Pages registradas como `Transient` no `MauiProgram.cs`.
- ServiÃ§os e repositÃ³rios registrados como `Singleton` no `MauiProgram.cs`.
- As Pages recebem a respectiva ViewModel via injeÃ§Ã£o de dependÃªncia no construtor e definem `BindingContext = viewModel`.

## 3. Regras de Nomenclatura e Idioma
- **CÃ³digo:** 100% em inglÃªs (classes, mÃ©todos, propriedades, variÃ¡veis, arquivos, namespaces).
- **Interface com UsuÃ¡rio (UI):** 100% em PortuguÃªs do Brasil (pt-BR).
- **ConvenÃ§Ãµes:**
  - Classes e MÃ©todos: PascalCase (`DeviceDetailViewModel`, `GetDevicesAsync`).
  - Campos privados: camelCase com prefixo `_` (`_isBusy`, `_devices`).
  - Comandos: sufixo gerado `Command` (`EntrarCommand`).

## 4. Tokens de Cor (Resources/Styles/Colors.xaml)
- `Primary`: `#00326F` (Azul institucional)
- `Accent`: `#FC6100` (Laranja de destaque/aÃ§Ã£o)
- `Background`: `#F6F9FF` (Fundo geral do app)
- `Surface`: `#EEF4FC` (Fundo de cards e superfÃ­cies elevadas)
- `SurfaceVariant`: `#E8EEF6`
- `OnSurface`: `#161C22` (Texto principal sobre Surface)
- `OnSurfaceVariant`: `#424751` (Texto secundÃ¡rio/muted)
- `Outline`: `#737783` (Bordas e divisores padrÃ£o)
- `AccentContainer`: `#FFDBCD`
- `OnAccentContainer`: `#360F00`
- `AccentText`: `#A43D00`
- `AuthText`: `#0F172A` (Texto escuro telas de auth)
- `AuthMuted`: `#64748B` (Texto de apoio auth)
- `AuthBorder`: `#E2E8F0` (Borda de inputs e cards de auth)
- `Error`: `#BA1A1A`
- `ErrorBg`: `#FEF2F2`
- `ErrorBorder`: `#FECACA`
- `LogoBlue`: `#034899`
- `White`: `#FFFFFF`

## 5. Tipografia (Inter)
Fontes estÃ¡ticas Inter registradas via aliases:
- `InterRegular`: Fonte padrÃ£o de corpo.
- `InterMedium`: Textos com Ãªnfase mÃ©dia, tÃ­tulos mÃ©dios e botÃµes.
- `InterSemiBold`: TÃ­tulos fortes e overlines.
- `InterBold`: Destaques numÃ©ricos e tÃ­tulos principais.
- *Fallback:* `OpenSansRegular`, `OpenSansSemibold`.

### Estilos PrÃ©-definidos (`Styles.xaml`)
- **PadrÃ£o (`Label`):** InterRegular, 14, `OnSurface`.
- **`TitleAuth`:** InterSemiBold, 24, `AuthText`.
- **`Heading`:** InterMedium, 18.
- **`BodySmall`:** 13, `OnSurfaceVariant`.
- **`Caption`:** InterMedium, 12.
- **`Overline`:** InterSemiBold, 11, `CharacterSpacing=0.55`, `TextTransform=Uppercase`, `OnSurfaceVariant`.

## 6. Formas e DimensÃµes PadrÃ£o
- **Raios de Borda (CornerRadius):**
  - Pequeno / BotÃµes e Cards padrÃ£o: `8`
  - MÃ©dio / Inputs e DiÃ¡logos: `12`
  - Grande / Auth Cards e Modais: `16`
- **Alturas Fixas:**
  - BotÃ£o / Input: `48`
  - Header: `64`
  - Tab Bar: `80`

## 7. Regra Fundamental
> **use sempre os estilos de Styles.xaml e os tokens de Colors.xaml; nunca coloque cores ou tamanhos fixos nas telas**.
- Nunca use `Frame` (obsoleto); use sempre `Border`.
- NÃ£o instale Tailwind ou bibliotecas web.

# Set the merge driver for project and solution files
#
# Merging from the command prompt will add diff markers to the files if there
# are conflicts (Merging from VS is not affected by the settings below, in VS
# the diff markers are never inserted). Diff markers may cause the following 
# file extensions to fail to load in VS. An alternative would be to treat
# these files as binary and thus will always conflict and require user
# intervention with every merge. To do so, just uncomment the entries below
###############################################################################

#*.csproj    merge=binary

#*.modelproj merge=binary

--- FILE: App.xaml ---
<?xml version = "1.0" encoding = "UTF-8" ?>
<Application xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/dotnet/2021/maui"
             xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2009/xaml"
             xmlns:local="clr-namespace:Venther.App"
             x:Class="Venther.App.App">
    <Application.Resources>
        <ResourceDictionary>
            <ResourceDictionary.MergedDictionaries>
                <ResourceDictionary Source="Resources/Styles/Colors.xaml" />
                <ResourceDictionary Source="Resources/Styles/Styles.xaml" />
            </ResourceDictionary.MergedDictionaries>
        </ResourceDictionary>
    </Application.Resources>
</Application>
--- FILE: App.xaml.cs ---
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;

namespace Venther.App
{
    public partial class App : Application
    {
        public App()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        protected override Window CreateWindow(IActivationState? activationState)
        {
            return new Window(new AppShell());
        }
    }
}
--- FILE: AppShell.xaml ---
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<Shell
    x:Class="Venther.App.AppShell"
    xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/dotnet/2021/maui"
    xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2009/xaml"
    xmlns:auth="clr-namespace:Venther.App.Views.Auth"
    xmlns:home="clr-namespace:Venther.App.Views.Home"
    xmlns:alerts="clr-namespace:Venther.App.Views.Alerts"
    xmlns:workorders="clr-namespace:Venther.App.Views.WorkOrders"
    Title="Venther"
    FlyoutBehavior="Disabled"
    Shell.TabBarBackgroundColor="{StaticResource Background}"
    Shell.TabBarForegroundColor="{StaticResource Primary}"
    Shell.TabBarTitleColor="{StaticResource Primary}"
    Shell.TabBarUnselectedColor="{StaticResource OnSurfaceVariant}">

    <ShellContent
        Route="login"
        ContentTemplate="{DataTemplate auth:LoginPage}"
        Shell.FlyoutItemIsVisible="False"
        Shell.TabBarIsVisible="False" />

    <TabBar Route="main">
        <!-- TODO: Substituir Ã­cones de abas pelos SVGs Lucide (Home, Bell/Alert, ClipboardList/Wrench) -->
        <Tab Title="InÃ­cio" Route="home" Icon="dotnet_bot.png">
            <ShellContent ContentTemplate="{DataTemplate home:HomePage}" />
        </Tab>
        <Tab Title="Alertas" Route="alerts" Icon="dotnet_bot.png">
            <ShellContent ContentTemplate="{DataTemplate alerts:AlertsPage}" />
        </Tab>
        <Tab Title="Chamados" Route="workorders" Icon="dotnet_bot.png">
            <ShellContent ContentTemplate="{DataTemplate workorders:WorkOrdersPage}" />
        </Tab>
    </TabBar>

</Shell>
--- FILE: AppShell.xaml.cs ---
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Venther.App.Helpers;
using Venther.App.Views.Alerts;
using Venther.App.Views.Auth;
using Venther.App.Views.Devices;
using Venther.App.Views.Home;
using Venther.App.Views.WorkOrders;

namespace Venther.App;

public partial class AppShell : Shell
{
    public AppShell()
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        Routing.RegisterRoute(Routes.DeviceDetail, typeof(DeviceDetailPage));
        Routing.RegisterRoute(Routes.WorkOrderDetail, typeof(WorkOrderDetailPage));
        Routing.RegisterRoute(Routes.RegisterDevice, typeof(RegisterDevicePage));
        Routing.RegisterRoute(Routes.DeviceConnection, typeof(DeviceConnectionPage));
        Routing.RegisterRoute(Routes.DeviceActivated, typeof(DeviceActivatedPage));

        Routing.RegisterRoute(Routes.ForgotPassword, typeof(ForgotPasswordPage));
        Routing.RegisterRoute(Routes.CheckEmail, typeof(CheckEmailPage));
        Routing.RegisterRoute(Routes.ResetPassword, typeof(ResetPasswordPage));
        Routing.RegisterRoute(Routes.PasswordResetSuccess, typeof(PasswordResetSuccessPage));
    }
}

--- FILE: Controls/AlertCard.xaml ---
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8" ?>
<ContentView xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/dotnet/2021/maui"
             xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2009/xaml"
             xmlns:toolkit="http://schemas.microsoft.com/dotnet/2022/maui/toolkit"
             x:Class="Venther.App.Controls.AlertCard"
             x:Name="CardRoot">

    <Border BackgroundColor="{StaticResource Surface}"
            StrokeShape="RoundRectangle 12"
            StrokeThickness="1"
            Stroke="{StaticResource AuthBorder}"
            Padding="16">
        <Border.Shadow>
            <Shadow Brush="Black" Opacity="0.05" Offset="0, 2" Radius="4" />
        </Border.Shadow>
        <Border.GestureRecognizers>
            <TapGestureRecognizer Tapped="OnCardTapped" />
        </Border.GestureRecognizers>

        <Grid ColumnDefinitions="Auto, *, Auto" ColumnSpacing="12" VerticalOptions="Center">
            <!-- Icon in Circle (Col 0) -->
            <Border x:Name="IconCircle"
                    Grid.Column="0"
                    WidthRequest="42"
                    HeightRequest="42"
                    StrokeShape="RoundRectangle 21"
                    StrokeThickness="0"
                    VerticalOptions="Center"
                    HorizontalOptions="Center">
                <Image x:Name="AlertIconImage"
                       WidthRequest="20"
                       HeightRequest="20"
                       Aspect="AspectFit"
                       HorizontalOptions="Center"
                       VerticalOptions="Center" />
            </Border>

            <!-- Middle Details (Col 1) -->
            <VerticalStackLayout Grid.Column="1" Spacing="4" VerticalOptions="Center">
                <!-- Title & Severity / Resolved Badge -->
                <Grid ColumnDefinitions="*, Auto" ColumnSpacing="6" VerticalOptions="Center">
                    <Label Grid.Column="0"
                           Text="{Binding Alert.Title, Source={x:Reference CardRoot}}"
                           FontFamily="InterMedium"
                           FontSize="15"
                           TextColor="{StaticResource OnSurface}"
                           LineBreakMode="TailTruncation"
                           VerticalOptions="Center" />

                    <!-- Badge (Resolvido / CrÃ­tico / Aviso) -->
                    <Border x:Name="StatusBadgeBorder"
                            Grid.Column="1"
                            StrokeThickness="0"
                            StrokeShape="RoundRectangle 6"
                            Padding="6, 2"
                            VerticalOptions="Center">
                        <Label x:Name="StatusBadgeLabel"
                               FontFamily="InterMedium"
                               FontSize="11"
                               VerticalOptions="Center" />
                    </Border>
                </Grid>

                <!-- Equipment & ID -->
                <Label Text="{Binding Alert.DeviceInfo, Source={x:Reference CardRoot}}"
                       Style="{StaticResource BodySmall}"
                       TextColor="{StaticResource OnSurfaceVariant}"
                       LineBreakMode="TailTruncation" />

                <!-- Time / Timestamp -->
                <HorizontalStackLayout Spacing="4" VerticalOptions="Center">
                    <Image Source="clock.svg"
                           WidthRequest="12"
                           HeightRequest="12"
                           Aspect="AspectFit"
                           VerticalOptions="Center">
                        <Image.Behaviors>
                            <toolkit:IconTintColorBehavior TintColor="{StaticResource OnSurfaceVariant}" />
                        </Image.Behaviors>
                    </Image>
                    <Label x:Name="TimeLabel"
                           Style="{StaticResource Caption}"
                           TextColor="{StaticResource OnSurfaceVariant}"
                           VerticalOptions="Center" />
                </HorizontalStackLayout>
            </VerticalStackLayout>

            <!-- Action Button: "Ver" (Col 2) -->
            <Button Grid.Column="2"
                    Text="Ver"
                    Style="{StaticResource PrimaryButton}"
                    HeightRequest="36"
                    CornerRadius="8"
                    Padding="14, 0"
                    FontSize="13"
                    VerticalOptions="Center"
                    Clicked="OnActionClicked" />
        </Grid>
    </Border>
</ContentView>
--- FILE: Controls/AlertCard.xaml.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Windows.Input;
using CommunityToolkit.Maui.Behaviors;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Microsoft.Maui.Graphics;
using Venther.App.Helpers;
using Venther.App.Models;

namespace Venther.App.Controls;

public partial class AlertCard : ContentView
{
    public static readonly BindableProperty AlertProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(Alert),
            typeof(AlertItem),
            typeof(AlertCard),
            default(AlertItem),
            propertyChanged: (b, _, _) => ((AlertCard)b).UpdateVisuals());

    public static readonly BindableProperty ActionCommandProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(ActionCommand),
            typeof(ICommand),
            typeof(AlertCard),
            default(ICommand));

    public AlertItem? Alert
    {
        get => (AlertItem?)GetValue(AlertProperty);
        set => SetValue(AlertProperty, value);
    }

    public ICommand? ActionCommand
    {
        get => (ICommand?)GetValue(ActionCommandProperty);
        set => SetValue(ActionCommandProperty, value);
    }

    public AlertCard()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        UpdateVisuals();
    }

    private void UpdateVisuals()
    {
        if (Alert == null || IconCircle == null || AlertIconImage == null || StatusBadgeBorder == null || StatusBadgeLabel == null || TimeLabel == null)
            return;

        Color iconTint;

        if (Alert.IsResolved)
        {
            var greenBg = Color.FromArgb("#DCFCE7");
            var greenText = Color.FromArgb("#15803D");
            iconTint = Color.FromArgb("#16A34A");

            IconCircle.BackgroundColor = greenBg;
            AlertIconImage.Source = "check_circle.svg";

            StatusBadgeBorder.BackgroundColor = greenBg;
            StatusBadgeLabel.TextColor = greenText;
            StatusBadgeLabel.Text = "Resolvido";
        }
        else if (Alert.Severity == AlertSeverity.Critical)
        {
            var errorBg = GetThemeColor("ErrorBg", Color.FromArgb("#FEF2F2"));
            var errorText = GetThemeColor("Error", Color.FromArgb("#BA1A1A"));
            iconTint = errorText;

            IconCircle.BackgroundColor = errorBg;
            AlertIconImage.Source = "alert_triangle.svg";

            StatusBadgeBorder.BackgroundColor = errorBg;
            StatusBadgeLabel.TextColor = errorText;
            StatusBadgeLabel.Text = "CrÃ­tico";
        }
        else
        {
            var accentContainer = GetThemeColor("AccentContainer", Color.FromArgb("#FFDBCD"));
            var accentText = GetThemeColor("AccentText", Color.FromArgb("#A43D00"));
            iconTint = GetThemeColor("Accent", Color.FromArgb("#FC6100"));

            IconCircle.BackgroundColor = accentContainer;
            AlertIconImage.Source = "alert_circle.svg";

            StatusBadgeBorder.BackgroundColor = accentContainer;
            StatusBadgeLabel.TextColor = accentText;
            StatusBadgeLabel.Text = "Aviso";
        }

        // Apply IconTintColorBehavior
        AlertIconImage.Behaviors.Clear();
        AlertIconImage.Behaviors.Add(new IconTintColorBehavior { TintColor = iconTint });

        TimeLabel.Text = FormatRelativeTime(Alert.Date);
    }

    private string FormatRelativeTime(DateTime date)
    {
        var span = DateTime.Now - date;
        if (span.TotalMinutes < 1)
            return "Agora";
        if (span.TotalMinutes < 60)
            return $"HÃ¡ {(int)span.TotalMinutes} min";
        if (span.TotalHours < 24)
            return $"HÃ¡ {(int)span.TotalHours}h";
        if (span.TotalDays < 30)
            return $"HÃ¡ {(int)span.TotalDays}d";

        return date.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy");
    }

    private async void OnCardTapped(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        if (ActionCommand != null && ActionCommand.CanExecute(Alert))
        {
            ActionCommand.Execute(Alert);
            return;
        }

        if (Alert != null)
        {
            await Shell.Current.GoToAsync(Routes.DeviceDetail, new Dictionary<string, object>
            {
                ["Alert"] = Alert,
                ["AlertId"] = Alert.Id
            });
        }
    }

    private void OnActionClicked(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        OnCardTapped(sender, e);
    }

    private Color GetThemeColor(string key, Color fallback)
    {
        if (Application.Current?.Resources.TryGetValue(key, out var val) == true && val is Color color)
        {
            return color;
        }
        return fallback;
    }
}

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8" ?>
<ContentView xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/dotnet/2021/maui"
             xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2009/xaml"
             x:Class="Venther.App.Controls.AppEntry"
             x:Name="This">

    <VerticalStackLayout Spacing="6">
        <!-- Field Label -->
        <Label x:Name="FieldLabel"
               Text="{Binding Label, Source={x:Reference This}}"
               Style="{StaticResource BodySmall}"
               FontFamily="InterMedium"
               TextColor="{StaticResource AuthText}"
               IsVisible="False" />

        <!-- Input Container -->
        <Border x:Name="InputBorder"
                HeightRequest="48"
                StrokeShape="RoundRectangle 12"
                StrokeThickness="1"
                Stroke="{StaticResource AuthBorder}"
                BackgroundColor="{StaticResource White}"
                Padding="16, 0">
            <Border.GestureRecognizers>
                <TapGestureRecognizer Tapped="OnBorderTapped" />
            </Border.GestureRecognizers>
            <Grid ColumnDefinitions="*, Auto" VerticalOptions="Center">
                <!-- Text Entry -->
                <Entry x:Name="InnerEntry"
                       Grid.Column="0"
                       Text="{Binding Text, Source={x:Reference This}, Mode=TwoWay}"
                       Placeholder="{Binding Placeholder, Source={x:Reference This}}"
                       PlaceholderColor="{StaticResource AuthMuted}"
                       TextColor="{StaticResource AuthText}"
                       FontFamily="InterRegular"
                       FontSize="14"
                       VerticalOptions="Center" />

                <!-- Password Toggle Button -->
                <Border x:Name="PasswordToggleBorder"
                        Grid.Column="1"
                        IsVisible="False"
                        WidthRequest="36"
                        HeightRequest="36"
                        StrokeShape="RoundRectangle 8"
                        StrokeThickness="0"
                        BackgroundColor="Transparent"
                        VerticalOptions="Center"
                        HorizontalOptions="Center">
                    <Border.GestureRecognizers>
                        <TapGestureRecognizer Tapped="OnTogglePasswordTapped" />
                    </Border.GestureRecognizers>
                    <Grid WidthRequest="20" HeightRequest="20" HorizontalOptions="Center" VerticalOptions="Center">
                        <!-- Eye Open (Hide Password) -->
                        <Path x:Name="EyeOpenPath"
                              Aspect="Uniform"
                              Stroke="{StaticResource AuthMuted}"
                              StrokeThickness="1.5"
                              Fill="Transparent"
                              IsVisible="True"
                              Data="M 1,10 C 4,4 16,4 19,10 C 16,16 4,16 1,10 Z M 10,7.5 A 2.5,2.5 0 1 1 9.99,7.5 Z" />
                        <!-- Eye Closed (Show Password) -->
                        <Path x:Name="EyeClosedPath"
                              Aspect="Uniform"
                              Stroke="{StaticResource AuthMuted}"
                              StrokeThickness="1.5"
                              Fill="Transparent"
                              IsVisible="False"
                              Data="M 1,10 C 4,4 16,4 19,10 C 16,16 4,16 1,10 Z M 10,7.5 A 2.5,2.5 0 1 1 9.99,7.5 Z M 2,2 L 18,18" />
                    </Grid>
                </Border>
            </Grid>
        </Border>

        <!-- Error Message -->
        <Label x:Name="ErrorLabel"
               Text="{Binding ErrorMessage, Source={x:Reference This}}"
               Style="{StaticResource Caption}"
               TextColor="{StaticResource Error}"
               IsVisible="False" />
    </VerticalStackLayout>
</ContentView>
--- FILE: Controls/AppEntry.xaml.cs ---
using System;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Microsoft.Maui.Graphics;

namespace Venther.App.Controls;

public partial class AppEntry : ContentView
{
    private bool _isFocused;
    private bool _isPasswordRevealed;

    public static readonly BindableProperty TextProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(Text),
            typeof(string),
            typeof(AppEntry),
            default(string),
            BindingMode.TwoWay);

    public static readonly BindableProperty PlaceholderProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(Placeholder),
            typeof(string),
            typeof(AppEntry),
            string.Empty);

    public static readonly BindableProperty LabelProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(Label),
            typeof(string),
            typeof(AppEntry),
            string.Empty,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, n) => ((AppEntry)b).OnLabelChanged((string)n));

    public static readonly BindableProperty IsPasswordProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(IsPassword),
            typeof(bool),
            typeof(AppEntry),
            false,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, n) => ((AppEntry)b).OnIsPasswordChanged((bool)n));

    public static readonly BindableProperty KeyboardProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(Keyboard),
            typeof(Keyboard),
            typeof(AppEntry),
            Keyboard.Default,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, n) => ((AppEntry)b).InnerEntry.Keyboard = (Keyboard)n);

    public static readonly BindableProperty ReturnTypeProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(ReturnType),
            typeof(ReturnType),
            typeof(AppEntry),
            ReturnType.Default,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, n) => ((AppEntry)b).InnerEntry.ReturnType = (ReturnType)n);

    public static readonly BindableProperty HasErrorProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(HasError),
            typeof(bool),
            typeof(AppEntry),
            false,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, _) => ((AppEntry)b).UpdateVisualState());

    public static readonly BindableProperty ErrorMessageProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(ErrorMessage),
            typeof(string),
            typeof(AppEntry),
            string.Empty,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, _) => ((AppEntry)b).UpdateVisualState());

    public string? Text
    {
        get => (string?)GetValue(TextProperty);
        set => SetValue(TextProperty, value);
    }

    public string Placeholder
    {
        get => (string)GetValue(PlaceholderProperty);
        set => SetValue(PlaceholderProperty, value);
    }

    public string Label
    {
        get => (string)GetValue(LabelProperty);
        set => SetValue(LabelProperty, value);
    }

    public bool IsPassword
    {
        get => (bool)GetValue(IsPasswordProperty);
        set => SetValue(IsPasswordProperty, value);
    }

    public Keyboard Keyboard
    {
        get => (Keyboard)GetValue(KeyboardProperty);
        set => SetValue(KeyboardProperty, value);
    }

    public ReturnType ReturnType
    {
        get => (ReturnType)GetValue(ReturnTypeProperty);
        set => SetValue(ReturnTypeProperty, value);
    }

    public bool HasError
    {
        get => (bool)GetValue(HasErrorProperty);
        set => SetValue(HasErrorProperty, value);
    }

    public string ErrorMessage
    {
        get => (string)GetValue(ErrorMessageProperty);
        set => SetValue(ErrorMessageProperty, value);
    }

    public AppEntry()
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        InnerEntry.Focused += OnEntryFocused;
        InnerEntry.Unfocused += OnEntryUnfocused;

        UpdateVisualState();
    }

    private void OnEntryFocused(object? sender, FocusEventArgs e)
    {
        _isFocused = true;
        UpdateVisualState();
    }

    private void OnEntryUnfocused(object? sender, FocusEventArgs e)
    {
        _isFocused = false;
        UpdateVisualState();
    }

    private void OnBorderTapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
    {
        InnerEntry.Focus();
    }

    private void OnLabelChanged(string newLabel)
    {
        FieldLabel.IsVisible = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newLabel);
    }

    private void OnIsPasswordChanged(bool isPassword)
    {
        _isPasswordRevealed = false;
        InnerEntry.IsPassword = isPassword;
        PasswordToggleBorder.IsVisible = isPassword;
        EyeOpenPath.IsVisible = true;
        EyeClosedPath.IsVisible = false;
    }

    private void OnTogglePasswordTapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
    {
        _isPasswordRevealed = !_isPasswordRevealed;
        InnerEntry.IsPassword = !_isPasswordRevealed;
        EyeOpenPath.IsVisible = !_isPasswordRevealed;
        EyeClosedPath.IsVisible = _isPasswordRevealed;
    }

    private void UpdateVisualState()
    {
        if (HasError)
        {
            InputBorder.BackgroundColor = GetThemeColor("ErrorBg", Color.FromArgb("#FEF2F2"));
            InputBorder.Stroke = GetThemeBrush("ErrorBorder", Color.FromArgb("#FECACA"));
            InputBorder.StrokeThickness = 1;
            ErrorLabel.IsVisible = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ErrorMessage);
        }
        else if (_isFocused)
        {
            InputBorder.BackgroundColor = GetThemeColor("White", Colors.White);
            InputBorder.Stroke = GetThemeBrush("Primary", Color.FromArgb("#00326F"));
            InputBorder.StrokeThickness = 1.5;
            ErrorLabel.IsVisible = false;
        }
        else
        {
            InputBorder.BackgroundColor = GetThemeColor("White", Colors.White);
            InputBorder.Stroke = GetThemeBrush("AuthBorder", Color.FromArgb("#E2E8F0"));
            InputBorder.StrokeThickness = 1;
            ErrorLabel.IsVisible = false;
        }
    }

    private Color GetThemeColor(string key, Color fallback)
    {
        if (Application.Current?.Resources.TryGetValue(key, out var val) == true && val is Color color)
        {
            return color;
        }
        return fallback;
    }

    private Brush GetThemeBrush(string key, Color fallback)
    {
        if (Application.Current?.Resources.TryGetValue(key, out var val) == true)
        {
            if (val is Brush brush) return brush;
            if (val is Color color) return new SolidColorBrush(color);
        }
        return new SolidColorBrush(fallback);
    }
}

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8" ?>
<ContentView xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/dotnet/2021/maui"
             xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2009/xaml"
             xmlns:toolkit="http://schemas.microsoft.com/dotnet/2022/maui/toolkit"

    <Grid ColumnDefinitions="*, Auto"
          Padding="20, 0"
          VerticalOptions="Center">

        <!-- Left: Logo + App Name OR Screen Title -->

                    BackgroundColor="{StaticResource Surface}"
                    VerticalOptions="Center"
                    HorizontalOptions="Center"
                    IsVisible="False">
                <Border.GestureRecognizers>
                    <TapGestureRecognizer Tapped="OnSyncTapped" />

                       WidthRequest="18"
                       HeightRequest="18"
                       Aspect="AspectFit"
                       HorizontalOptions="Center"
                       VerticalOptions="Center">
                    <Image.Behaviors>
                        <toolkit:IconTintColorBehavior TintColor="{StaticResource OnSurfaceVariant}" />
                    </Image.Behaviors>
                </Image>
            </Border>

            <!-- Bell / Notifications Button -->
            <Border x:Name="BellButton"
                    WidthRequest="40"
                    HeightRequest="40"
                    StrokeShape="RoundRectangle 20"
                    StrokeThickness="0"
                    BackgroundColor="{StaticResource Surface}"
                    VerticalOptions="Center"
                    HorizontalOptions="Center">
                <Border.GestureRecognizers>
                    <TapGestureRecognizer Tapped="OnBellTapped" />
                </Border.GestureRecognizers>
                <Image Source="bell.svg"
                       WidthRequest="20"
                       HeightRequest="20"
                       Aspect="AspectFit"
                       HorizontalOptions="Center"
                       VerticalOptions="Center">
                    <Image.Behaviors>
                        <toolkit:IconTintColorBehavior TintColor="{StaticResource OnSurfaceVariant}" />
                    </Image.Behaviors>
                </Image>
            </Border>

            <!-- User Avatar -->
            <Border WidthRequest="40"
                    HeightRequest="40"
                    StrokeShape="RoundRectangle 20"
                    StrokeThickness="1"
                    Stroke="{StaticResource Outline}"
                    BackgroundColor="{StaticResource SurfaceVariant}"
                    VerticalOptions="Center"
                    HorizontalOptions="Center">
                <Image Source="user.svg"
                       WidthRequest="20"
                       HeightRequest="20"
                       Aspect="AspectFit"

                       VerticalOptions="Center">

                        <toolkit:IconTintColorBehavior TintColor="{StaticResource Primary}" />

                </Image>
            </Border>
        </HorizontalStackLayout>
    </Grid>
</ContentView>
--- FILE: Controls/AppHeader.xaml.cs ---
using System;
using System.Windows.Input;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;

namespace Venther.App.Controls;

public partial class AppHeader : ContentView
{
    public static readonly BindableProperty TitleProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(Title),
            typeof(string),
            typeof(AppHeader),
            default(string),
            propertyChanged: (b, _, n) => ((AppHeader)b).OnTitleChanged((string?)n));

    public static readonly BindableProperty ShowFilterButtonProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(ShowFilterButton),
            typeof(bool),
            typeof(AppHeader),
            false,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, n) => ((AppHeader)b).FilterButton.IsVisible = (bool)n);

    public static readonly BindableProperty FilterCommandProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(FilterCommand),
            typeof(ICommand),
            typeof(AppHeader),
            default(ICommand));

    public static readonly BindableProperty ShowSyncButtonProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(ShowSyncButton),
            typeof(bool),
            typeof(AppHeader),
            false,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, n) => ((AppHeader)b).SyncButton.IsVisible = (bool)n);

    public static readonly BindableProperty ShowBellButtonProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(ShowBellButton),
            typeof(bool),
            typeof(AppHeader),
            true,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, n) => ((AppHeader)b).BellButton.IsVisible = (bool)n);

    public static readonly BindableProperty SyncCommandProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(SyncCommand),
            typeof(ICommand),
            typeof(AppHeader),
            default(ICommand));

    public static readonly BindableProperty BellCommandProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(BellCommand),
            typeof(ICommand),
            typeof(AppHeader),
            default(ICommand));

    public string? Title
    {
        get => (string?)GetValue(TitleProperty);
        set => SetValue(TitleProperty, value);
    }

    public bool ShowFilterButton
    {
        get => (bool)GetValue(ShowFilterButtonProperty);
        set => SetValue(ShowFilterButtonProperty, value);
    }

    public ICommand? FilterCommand
    {
        get => (ICommand?)GetValue(FilterCommandProperty);
        set => SetValue(FilterCommandProperty, value);
    }

    public bool ShowSyncButton
    {
        get => (bool)GetValue(ShowSyncButtonProperty);
        set => SetValue(ShowSyncButtonProperty, value);
    }

    public bool ShowBellButton
    {
        get => (bool)GetValue(ShowBellButtonProperty);
        set => SetValue(ShowBellButtonProperty, value);
    }

    public ICommand? SyncCommand
    {
        get => (ICommand?)GetValue(SyncCommandProperty);
        set => SetValue(SyncCommandProperty, value);
    }

    public ICommand? BellCommand
    {
        get => (ICommand?)GetValue(BellCommandProperty);
        set => SetValue(BellCommandProperty, value);
    }

    public AppHeader()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
    }

    private void OnTitleChanged(string? newTitle)
    {
        bool hasTitle = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newTitle);
        TitleLabel.IsVisible = hasTitle;
        BrandingLayout.IsVisible = !hasTitle;
    }

    private void OnFilterTapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (FilterCommand?.CanExecute(null) == true)
            FilterCommand.Execute(null);
    }

    private void OnSyncTapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (SyncCommand?.CanExecute(null) == true)
        {
            SyncCommand.Execute(null);
        }
    }

    private async void OnBellTapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (BellCommand?.CanExecute(null) == true)
        {
            BellCommand.Execute(null);
            return;
        }

        // Navega diretamente para a aba de Alertas
        if (Shell.Current != null)
        {
            await Shell.Current.GoToAsync("//main/alerts");
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: Controls/DeviceCard.xaml.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Windows.Input;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Microsoft.Maui.Graphics;
using Venther.App.Helpers;
using Venther.App.Models;
using Device = Venther.App.Models.Device;

namespace Venther.App.Controls;

public partial class DeviceCard : ContentView
{
    public static readonly BindableProperty DeviceProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(Device),
            typeof(Device),
            typeof(DeviceCard),
            default(Device),
            propertyChanged: (b, _, _) => ((DeviceCard)b).OnDeviceChanged());

    public static readonly BindableProperty TapCommandProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(TapCommand),
            typeof(ICommand),
            typeof(DeviceCard),
            default(ICommand));

    public Device? Device
    {
        get => (Device?)GetValue(DeviceProperty);
        set => SetValue(DeviceProperty, value);
    }

    public ICommand? TapCommand
    {
        get => (ICommand?)GetValue(TapCommandProperty);
        set => SetValue(TapCommandProperty, value);
    }

    public DeviceCard()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        UpdateStatusBar();
    }

    private void OnDeviceChanged()
    {
        UpdateStatusBar();
    }

    private void UpdateStatusBar()
    {
        if (StatusBar == null)
            return;

        var status = Device?.Status ?? DeviceStatus.Normal;
        switch (status)
        {
            case DeviceStatus.Attention:
                StatusBar.Color = GetThemeColor("Accent", Color.FromArgb("#FC6100"));
                break;
            case DeviceStatus.Critical:
                StatusBar.Color = GetThemeColor("Error", Color.FromArgb("#BA1A1A"));
                break;
            case DeviceStatus.Normal:
            default:
                StatusBar.Color = Color.FromArgb("#16A34A");
                break;
        }
    }

    private async void OnCardTapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (TapCommand?.CanExecute(Device) == true)
        {
            TapCommand.Execute(Device);
            return;
        }

        if (Shell.Current != null && Device != null)
        {
            var navParams = new Dictionary<string, object>
            {
                ["Device"] = Device,
                ["DeviceId"] = Device.Id
            };
            await Shell.Current.GoToAsync(Routes.DeviceDetail, navParams);
        }
    }

    private Color GetThemeColor(string key, Color fallback)
    {
        if (Application.Current?.Resources.TryGetValue(key, out var val) == true && val is Color color)
        {
            return color;
        }
        return fallback;
    }
}

namespace Venther.App.Controls;

public partial class MetricCard : ContentView
{
    public static readonly BindableProperty TitleProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(nameof(Title), typeof(string), typeof(MetricCard), string.Empty);
    public static readonly BindableProperty ValueProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(nameof(Value), typeof(string), typeof(MetricCard), string.Empty);
    public static readonly BindableProperty UnitProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(nameof(Unit), typeof(string), typeof(MetricCard), string.Empty);
    public static readonly BindableProperty CaptionProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(nameof(Caption), typeof(string), typeof(MetricCard), string.Empty);
    public static readonly BindableProperty IconSourceProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(nameof(IconSource), typeof(ImageSource), typeof(MetricCard));
    public static readonly BindableProperty TrendIconSourceProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(nameof(TrendIconSource), typeof(ImageSource), typeof(MetricCard));
    public static readonly BindableProperty IsAlertProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(nameof(IsAlert), typeof(bool), typeof(MetricCard), false);

    public string Title { get => (string)GetValue(TitleProperty); set => SetValue(TitleProperty, value); }
    public string Value { get => (string)GetValue(ValueProperty); set => SetValue(ValueProperty, value); }
    public string Unit { get => (string)GetValue(UnitProperty); set => SetValue(UnitProperty, value); }
    public string Caption { get => (string)GetValue(CaptionProperty); set => SetValue(CaptionProperty, value); }
    public ImageSource? IconSource { get => (ImageSource?)GetValue(IconSourceProperty); set => SetValue(IconSourceProperty, value); }
    public ImageSource? TrendIconSource { get => (ImageSource?)GetValue(TrendIconSourceProperty); set => SetValue(TrendIconSourceProperty, value); }
    public bool IsAlert { get => (bool)GetValue(IsAlertProperty); set => SetValue(IsAlertProperty, value); }

    // Propriedades calculadas (estado normal x alerta)
    public string UnitText => string.IsNullOrEmpty(Unit) ? string.Empty : $" {Unit}";
    public Color IconColor => IsAlert ? Res("Accent") : Res("Primary");
    public Color ValueColor => IsAlert ? Res("Accent") : Res("OnSurface");
    public Color TrendColor => IsAlert ? Res("AccentText") : Res("SuccessText");

    public MetricCard() => InitializeComponent();

    protected override void OnPropertyChanged(string? propertyName = null)
    {
        base.OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
        if (propertyName == nameof(Unit))
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(UnitText));
        else if (propertyName == nameof(IsAlert))
        {
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IconColor));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ValueColor));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TrendColor));
        }
    }

    private static Color Res(string key) =>
        Application.Current?.Resources.TryGetValue(key, out var v) == true && v is Color c ? c : Colors.Black;
}
--- FILE: Controls/SegmentItem.cs ---
using System.ComponentModel;

namespace Venther.App.Controls;

public class SegmentItem : INotifyPropertyChanged
{
    private string _title = string.Empty;
    private int _count;
    private string _key = string.Empty;

    public string Title
    {
        get => _title;
        set
        {
            if (_title != value)
            {
                _title = value;
                PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(Title)));
            }
        }
    }

    public int Count
    {
        get => _count;
        set
        {
            if (_count != value)
            {
                _count = value;
                PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(Count)));
            }
        }
    }

    public string Key
    {
        get => _key;
        set
        {
            if (_key != value)
            {
                _key = value;
                PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(Key)));
            }
        }
    }

    public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

    public SegmentItem() { }

    public SegmentItem(string title, int count = 0, string key = "")
    {
        Title = title;
        Count = count;
        Key = string.IsNullOrEmpty(key) ? title : key;
    }
}

        <Grid x:Name="SegmentsGrid"
              VerticalOptions="Fill"
              HorizontalOptions="Fill" />
    </Border>
</ContentView>
--- FILE: Controls/SegmentedControl.xaml.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Windows.Input;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Microsoft.Maui.Graphics;

namespace Venther.App.Controls;

public partial class SegmentedControl : ContentView
{
    public static readonly BindableProperty ItemsSourceProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(ItemsSource),
            typeof(IEnumerable<SegmentItem>),
            typeof(SegmentedControl),
            default(IEnumerable<SegmentItem>),
            propertyChanged: (b, _, _) => ((SegmentedControl)b).RenderSegments());

    public static readonly BindableProperty SelectedIndexProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(SelectedIndex),
            typeof(int),
            typeof(SegmentedControl),
            0,
            BindingMode.TwoWay,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, _) => ((SegmentedControl)b).UpdateSelection());

    public static readonly BindableProperty SelectionChangedCommandProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(SelectionChangedCommand),
            typeof(ICommand),
            typeof(SegmentedControl),
            default(ICommand));

    public IEnumerable<SegmentItem>? ItemsSource
    {
        get => (IEnumerable<SegmentItem>?)GetValue(ItemsSourceProperty);
        set => SetValue(ItemsSourceProperty, value);
    }

    public int SelectedIndex
    {
        get => (int)GetValue(SelectedIndexProperty);
        set => SetValue(SelectedIndexProperty, value);
    }

    public ICommand? SelectionChangedCommand
    {
        get => (ICommand?)GetValue(SelectionChangedCommandProperty);
        set => SetValue(SelectionChangedCommandProperty, value);
    }

    public SegmentedControl()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
    }

    public void Refresh()
    {
        RenderSegments();
    }

    private void RenderSegments()
    {
        SegmentsGrid.Children.Clear();
        SegmentsGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Clear();

        if (ItemsSource == null)
            return;

        var items = ItemsSource.ToList();
        if (items.Count == 0)
            return;

        for (int i = 0; i < items.Count; i++)
        {
            SegmentsGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition(GridLength.Star));

            var item = items[i];
            int index = i;
            bool isSelected = (index == SelectedIndex);

            var pill = CreateSegmentPill(item, index, isSelected);
            Grid.SetColumn(pill, index);
            SegmentsGrid.Children.Add(pill);
        }
    }

    private View CreateSegmentPill(SegmentItem item, int index, bool isSelected)
    {
        var border = new Border
        {
            StrokeThickness = 0,
            StrokeShape = new Microsoft.Maui.Controls.Shapes.RoundRectangle { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(8) },
            BackgroundColor = isSelected ? GetThemeColor("White", Colors.White) : Colors.Transparent,
            VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Fill,
            HorizontalOptions = LayoutOptions.Fill
        };

        if (isSelected)
        {
            border.Shadow = new Shadow
            {
                Brush = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Black),
                Opacity = 0.08f,
                Offset = new Point(0, 1),
                Radius = 2
            };
        }

        var stack = new HorizontalStackLayout
        {
            Spacing = 4,
            HorizontalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center,
            VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center
        };

        var titleLabel = new Label
        {
            Text = item.Title,
            FontFamily = isSelected ? "InterSemiBold" : "InterRegular",
            FontSize = 13,
            TextColor = isSelected ? GetThemeColor("OnSurface", Color.FromArgb("#161C22")) : GetThemeColor("OnSurfaceVariant", Color.FromArgb("#424751")),
            VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center
        };
        stack.Children.Add(titleLabel);

        // Counter badge
        if (item.Count >= 0)
        {
            var badgeBorder = new Border
            {
                StrokeThickness = 0,
                StrokeShape = new Microsoft.Maui.Controls.Shapes.RoundRectangle { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(9) },
                BackgroundColor = isSelected ? GetThemeColor("Surface", Color.FromArgb("#EEF4FC")) : Colors.Transparent,
                Padding = new Thickness(6, 1),
                VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center
            };

            var countLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = item.Count.ToString(),
                FontFamily = "InterBold",
                FontSize = 11,
                TextColor = isSelected ? GetThemeColor("Primary", Color.FromArgb("#00326F")) : GetThemeColor("OnSurfaceVariant", Color.FromArgb("#424751")),
                VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center
            };
            badgeBorder.Content = countLabel;
            stack.Children.Add(badgeBorder);
        }

        border.Content = stack;

        var tapGesture = new TapGestureRecognizer();
        tapGesture.Tapped += (s, e) =>
        {
            if (SelectedIndex != index)
            {
                SelectedIndex = index;
                SelectionChangedCommand?.Execute(item);
            }
        };
        border.GestureRecognizers.Add(tapGesture);

        return border;
    }

    private void UpdateSelection()
    {
        RenderSegments();
    }

    private Color GetThemeColor(string key, Color fallback)
    {
        if (Application.Current?.Resources.TryGetValue(key, out var val) == true && val is Color color)
        {
            return color;
        }
        return fallback;
    }
}

using System;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Microsoft.Maui.Graphics;
using Venther.App.Models;

namespace Venther.App.Controls;

public partial class StatusBadge : ContentView
{
    public static readonly BindableProperty StatusProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(Status),
            typeof(DeviceStatus),
            typeof(StatusBadge),
            DeviceStatus.Normal,
            propertyChanged: (b, _, _) => ((StatusBadge)b).UpdateBadge());

    public DeviceStatus Status
    {
        get => (DeviceStatus)GetValue(StatusProperty);
        set => SetValue(StatusProperty, value);
    }

    public StatusBadge()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        UpdateBadge();
    }

    private void UpdateBadge()
    {
        switch (Status)
        {
            case DeviceStatus.Attention:
                BadgeBorder.BackgroundColor = GetThemeColor("AccentContainer", Color.FromArgb("#FFDBCD"));
                BadgeDot.Color = GetThemeColor("Accent", Color.FromArgb("#FC6100"));
                BadgeLabel.TextColor = GetThemeColor("AccentText", Color.FromArgb("#A43D00"));
                BadgeLabel.Text = "AtenÃ§Ã£o";
                break;

            case DeviceStatus.Critical:
                BadgeBorder.BackgroundColor = GetThemeColor("ErrorBg", Color.FromArgb("#FEF2F2"));
                BadgeDot.Color = GetThemeColor("Error", Color.FromArgb("#BA1A1A"));
                BadgeLabel.TextColor = GetThemeColor("Error", Color.FromArgb("#BA1A1A"));
                BadgeLabel.Text = "CrÃ­tico";
                break;

            case DeviceStatus.Normal:
            default:
                BadgeBorder.BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("#DCFCE7");
                BadgeDot.Color = Color.FromArgb("#16A34A");
                BadgeLabel.TextColor = Color.FromArgb("#15803D");
                BadgeLabel.Text = "Normal";
                break;
        }
    }

    private Color GetThemeColor(string key, Color fallback)
    {
        if (Application.Current?.Resources.TryGetValue(key, out var val) == true && val is Color color)
        {
            return color;
        }
        return fallback;
    }
}

--- FILE: Controls/WorkOrderCard.xaml.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Windows.Input;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Microsoft.Maui.Graphics;
using Venther.App.Helpers;
using Venther.App.Models;

namespace Venther.App.Controls;

public partial class WorkOrderCard : ContentView
{
    // â”€â”€ Bindable properties â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    public static readonly BindableProperty WorkOrderProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(WorkOrder),
            typeof(WorkOrder),
            typeof(WorkOrderCard),
            default(WorkOrder),
            propertyChanged: (b, _, _) => ((WorkOrderCard)b).UpdateVisuals());

    public static readonly BindableProperty ActionCommandProperty =
        BindableProperty.Create(
            nameof(ActionCommand),
            typeof(ICommand),
            typeof(WorkOrderCard),
            default(ICommand));

    public WorkOrder? WorkOrder
    {
        get => (WorkOrder?)GetValue(WorkOrderProperty);
        set => SetValue(WorkOrderProperty, value);
    }

    public ICommand? ActionCommand
    {
        get => (ICommand?)GetValue(ActionCommandProperty);
        set => SetValue(ActionCommandProperty, value);
    }

    // â”€â”€ Constructor & visuals â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    public WorkOrderCard()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        UpdateVisuals();
    }

    private void UpdateVisuals()
    {
        if (WorkOrder == null) return;

        switch (WorkOrder.Status)
        {
            case WorkOrderStatus.InProgress:
                ShowInProgress();
                break;

            case WorkOrderStatus.Done:
                ShowScheduled(isDone: true);
                break;

            default:
                ShowScheduled(isDone: false);
                break;
        }
    }

    // â”€â”€ InProgress variant â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    private void ShowInProgress()
    {
        InProgressLayout.IsVisible = true;
        ScheduledLayout.IsVisible = false;

        var wo = WorkOrder!;
        StatusBar.Color = GetThemeColor("Accent", Color.FromArgb("#FC6100"));

        InProgressIdLabel.Text = wo.Id;
        InProgressTitleLabel.Text = wo.Title;
        InProgressDeviceLabel.Text = wo.DeviceName;
        InProgressLocationLabel.Text = wo.Location;
        TelemetryLabel.Text = wo.TelemetrySnippet ?? string.Empty;
        TechnicianLabel.Text = wo.TechnicianName ?? string.Empty;

        TelemetryBorder.IsVisible = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(wo.TelemetrySnippet);

        StartPulseAnimation();
    }

    private async void StartPulseAnimation()
    {
        // Simple scale pulse on the dot to suggest "live" state
        while (InProgressLayout.IsVisible && WorkOrder?.Status == WorkOrderStatus.InProgress)
        {
            await PulseDot.ScaleTo(1.5, 600, Easing.SinInOut);
            await PulseDot.ScaleTo(1.0, 600, Easing.SinInOut);
        }
    }

    // â”€â”€ Scheduled / Done variant â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    private void ShowScheduled(bool isDone)
    {
        InProgressLayout.IsVisible = false;
        ScheduledLayout.IsVisible = true;

        var wo = WorkOrder!;

        if (isDone)
        {
            // Green bar + "ConcluÃ­da" badge
            StatusBar.Color = Color.FromArgb("#16A34A");

            ScheduledStatusBadge.BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("#DCFCE7");
            ScheduledStatusLabel.Text = "ConcluÃ­da";
            ScheduledStatusLabel.TextColor = Color.FromArgb("#15803D");

            ScheduledActionButton.Text = "Ver detalhes";
            ScheduledActionButton.BackgroundColor = GetThemeColor("Surface", Color.FromArgb("#EEF4FC"));
            ScheduledActionButton.TextColor = GetThemeColor("OnSurface", Color.FromArgb("#161C22"));
        }
        else
        {
            // Outline / neutral bar + "Agendada" badge
            StatusBar.Color = GetThemeColor("Outline", Color.FromArgb("#737783"));

            ScheduledStatusBadge.BackgroundColor = GetThemeColor("SurfaceVariant", Color.FromArgb("#E8EEF6"));
            ScheduledStatusLabel.Text = "Agendada";
            ScheduledStatusLabel.TextColor = GetThemeColor("OnSurfaceVariant", Color.FromArgb("#424751"));

            ScheduledActionButton.Text = "Iniciar OS";
            ScheduledActionButton.BackgroundColor = GetThemeColor("Primary", Color.FromArgb("#00326F"));
            ScheduledActionButton.TextColor = GetThemeColor("White", Colors.White);
        }

        ScheduledIdLabel.Text = wo.Id;
        ScheduledTitleLabel.Text = wo.Title;
        ScheduledDeviceLabel.Text = wo.DeviceName;
        ScheduledLocationLabel.Text = wo.Location;
        ScheduledDateLabel.Text = wo.ScheduledDateLabel ?? wo.Date.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy");
    }

    // â”€â”€ Event handlers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    private async void OnCardTapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
        => await NavigateToDetail();

    private async void OnActionClicked(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        => await NavigateToDetail();

    private async System.Threading.Tasks.Task NavigateToDetail()
    {
        if (ActionCommand != null && ActionCommand.CanExecute(WorkOrder))
        {
            ActionCommand.Execute(WorkOrder);
            return;
        }

        if (WorkOrder != null)
        {
            await Shell.Current.GoToAsync(Routes.WorkOrderDetail, new Dictionary<string, object>
            {
                ["WorkOrderId"] = WorkOrder.Id,
                ["WorkOrder"] = WorkOrder
            });
        }
    }

    // â”€â”€ Helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    private Color GetThemeColor(string key, Color fallback)
    {
        if (Application.Current?.Resources.TryGetValue(key, out var val) == true && val is Color color)
            return color;
        return fallback;
    }
}

--- FILE: Helpers/TrendChartDrawable.cs ---
using Microsoft.Maui.Graphics;

namespace Venther.App.Helpers;

public class TrendChartDrawable : IDrawable
{
    public IReadOnlyList<double> Values { get; set; } = Array.Empty<double>();

    private static readonly Color Line = Color.FromArgb("#00326F");
    private static readonly Color Dot = Color.FromArgb("#FC6100");
    private static readonly Color Grid = Color.FromArgb("#C3C6D3");

    public void Draw(ICanvas canvas, RectF dirtyRect)
    {
        if (Values.Count < 2) return;

        const float padX = 8f, padTop = 16f, padBottom = 8f;
        float w = dirtyRect.Width - padX * 2;
        float h = dirtyRect.Height - padTop - padBottom;
        double min = Values.Min(), max = Values.Max();
        double range = Math.Max(max - min, 0.1);

        PointF P(int i) => new(
            padX + w * i / (Values.Count - 1),
            padTop + h * (float)(1 - (Values[i] - min) / range));

        // Grade horizontal tracejada
        canvas.StrokeColor = Grid;
        canvas.StrokeSize = 1;
        canvas.StrokeDashPattern = new float[] { 3, 4 };
        for (int g = 0; g < 3; g++)
        {
            float y = padTop + h * g / 2f;
            canvas.DrawLine(padX, y, padX + w, y);
        }
        canvas.StrokeDashPattern = null;

        // Curva suave (Catmull-Rom convertida em Bézier)
        var pts = Enumerable.Range(0, Values.Count).Select(P).ToArray();
        var line = new PathF(pts[0]);
        for (int i = 0; i < pts.Length - 1; i++)
        {
            var p0 = i > 0 ? pts[i - 1] : pts[i];
            var p1 = pts[i];
            var p2 = pts[i + 1];
            var p3 = i + 2 < pts.Length ? pts[i + 2] : p2;
            var c1 = new PointF(p1.X + (p2.X - p0.X) / 6f, p1.Y + (p2.Y - p0.Y) / 6f);
            var c2 = new PointF(p2.X - (p3.X - p1.X) / 6f, p2.Y - (p3.Y - p1.Y) / 6f);
            line.CurveTo(c1, c2, p2);
        }

        // Área com gradiente sob a curva
        var fill = new PathF(line);
        fill.LineTo(pts[^1].X, padTop + h);
        fill.LineTo(pts[0].X, padTop + h);
        fill.Close();
        var gradient = new LinearGradientPaint(
            new[]
            {
                new PaintGradientStop(0f, Line.WithAlpha(0.28f)),
                new PaintGradientStop(1f, Line.WithAlpha(0f))
            },
            new Point(0, 0), new Point(0, 1));
        canvas.SetFillPaint(gradient, new RectF(0, padTop, dirtyRect.Width, h));
        canvas.FillPath(fill);

        // Linha
        canvas.StrokeColor = Line;
        canvas.StrokeSize = 2;
        canvas.StrokeLineCap = LineCap.Round;
        canvas.DrawPath(line);

        // Ponto final destacado (laranja com halo)
        var last = pts[^1];
        canvas.FillColor = Dot.WithAlpha(0.25f);
        canvas.FillCircle(last.X, last.Y, 7f);
        canvas.FillColor = Dot;
        canvas.FillCircle(last.X, last.Y, 4f);
    }
}
--- FILE: Helpers/Validators.cs ---
using System.Net.Mail;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace Venther.App.Helpers;

public static partial class Validators
{
    private static readonly Regex PasswordRegex = new(@"^(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*[^A-Za-z0-9]).{8,}$", RegexOptions.Compiled);

    public static bool IsValidEmail(string? email)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email))
            return false;

        return MailAddress.TryCreate(email.Trim(), out _);
    }

    public static bool IsValidPassword(string? password)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(password))
            return false;

        return PasswordRegex.IsMatch(password);
    }
}
--- FILE: MauiProgram.cs ---
using CommunityToolkit.Maui;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using Venther.App.Services.Interfaces;
using Venther.App.Services.Mocks;
using Venther.App.ViewModels;
using Venther.App.Views.Alerts;
using Venther.App.Views.Auth;
using Venther.App.Views.Devices;
using Venther.App.Views.Home;
using Venther.App.Views.WorkOrders;

namespace Venther.App;

public static class MauiProgram
{
    public static MauiApp CreateMauiApp()
    {
        var builder = MauiApp.CreateBuilder();
        builder
            .UseMauiApp<App>()
            .UseMauiCommunityToolkit()
            .ConfigureFonts(fonts =>
            {
                fonts.AddFont("Inter_24pt-Regular.ttf", "InterRegular");
                fonts.AddFont("Inter_24pt-Medium.ttf", "InterMedium");
                fonts.AddFont("Inter_24pt-SemiBold.ttf", "InterSemiBold");
                fonts.AddFont("Inter_24pt-Bold.ttf", "InterBold");
                fonts.AddFont("OpenSans-Regular.ttf", "OpenSansRegular");
                fonts.AddFont("OpenSans-Semibold.ttf", "OpenSansSemibold");
            });

#if ANDROID
        Microsoft.Maui.Handlers.EntryHandler.Mapper.AppendToMapping("NoUnderline", (handler, view) =>
        {
            handler.PlatformView.Background = null;
            handler.PlatformView.SetBackgroundColor(Android.Graphics.Color.Transparent);
        });
#endif

        // Mock Services (Singleton)
        builder.Services.AddSingleton<IAuthService, MockAuthService>();
        builder.Services.AddSingleton<IDeviceService, MockDeviceService>();
        builder.Services.AddSingleton<IWorkOrderService, MockWorkOrderService>();
        builder.Services.AddSingleton<IAlertService, MockAlertService>();

        // ViewModels & Pages (Transient)
        builder.Services.AddTransient<LoginPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<LoginViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<ForgotPasswordPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<ForgotPasswordViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<CheckEmailPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<CheckEmailViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<ResetPasswordPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<ResetPasswordViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<PasswordResetSuccessPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<PasswordResetSuccessViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<HomePage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<HomeViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<AlertsPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<AlertsViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<WorkOrdersPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<WorkOrdersViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<WorkOrderDetailPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<WorkOrderDetailViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<DeviceDetailPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<DeviceDetailViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<RegisterDevicePage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<RegisterDeviceViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<DeviceConnectionPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<DeviceConnectionViewModel>();

        builder.Services.AddTransient<DeviceActivatedPage>();
        builder.Services.AddTransient<DeviceActivatedViewModel>();

#if DEBUG
        builder.Logging.AddDebug();
#endif

        return builder.Build();
    }
}

using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;

// To learn more about WinUI, the WinUI project structure,
// and more about our project templates, see: http://aka.ms/winui-project-info.

namespace Venther.App.WinUI
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Provides application-specific behavior to supplement the default Application class.
    /// </summary>
    public partial class App : MauiWinUIApplication
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Initializes the singleton application object.  This is the first line of authored code
        /// executed, and as such is the logical equivalent of main() or WinMain().
        /// </summary>
        public App()
        {
            this.InitializeComponent();
        }

        protected override MauiApp CreateMauiApp() => MauiProgram.CreateMauiApp();
    }

}

this directory (and child directories). Deployment of the asset to your application
is automatically handled by the following `MauiAsset` Build Action within your `.csproj`.

	<MauiAsset Include="Resources\Raw\**" LogicalName="%(RecursiveDir)%(Filename)%(Extension)" />

These files will be deployed with your package and will be accessible using Essentials:

	async Task LoadMauiAsset()
	{
		using var stream = await FileSystem.OpenAppPackageFileAsync("AboutAssets.txt");
		using var reader = new StreamReader(stream);

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Venther.App.Models;
using Venther.App.Services.Interfaces;

namespace Venther.App.Services.Mocks;

public class MockAlertService : IAlertService
{
    private readonly List<AlertItem> _alerts = new()
    {
        new AlertItem
        {
            Id = "ALT-001",
            Title = "Filtro saturado detectado",
            DeviceInfo = "Self Contained - Sala de Servidores",
            Severity = AlertSeverity.Warning,
            Date = DateTime.Now.AddHours(-2),
            IsResolved = false
        },
        new AlertItem
        {
            Id = "ALT-002",
            Title = "PressÃ£o alta de descarga",
            DeviceInfo = "VRF Condensadora 02",
            Severity = AlertSeverity.Critical,
            Date = DateTime.Now.AddHours(-1),
            IsResolved = false
        },
        new AlertItem
        {
            Id = "ALT-003",
            Title = "VariaÃ§Ã£o tÃ©rmica alÃ©m do limite",
            DeviceInfo = "Chiller Principal - Ala Sul",
            Severity = AlertSeverity.Warning,
            Date = DateTime.Now.AddDays(-1),
            IsResolved = true
        }
    };

    public Task<IReadOnlyList<AlertItem>> GetAlertsAsync()
    {
        return Task.FromResult<IReadOnlyList<AlertItem>>(_alerts.AsReadOnly());
    }

    public Task<AlertItem?> GetAlertByIdAsync(string id)
    {
        var alert = _alerts.FirstOrDefault(a => string.Equals(a.Id, id, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        return Task.FromResult(alert);
    }

    public Task<bool> ResolveAlertAsync(string id)
    {
        var alert = _alerts.FirstOrDefault(a => string.Equals(a.Id, id, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        if (alert == null)
            return Task.FromResult(false);

        alert.IsResolved = true;
        return Task.FromResult(true);
    }
}
--- FILE: Services/Mocks/MockAuthService.cs ---
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Venther.App.Services.Interfaces;

namespace Venther.App.Services.Mocks;

public class MockAuthService : IAuthService
{
    private bool _isAuthenticated;

    public Task<bool> LoginAsync(string email, string password)
    {
        _isAuthenticated = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(password);
        return Task.FromResult(_isAuthenticated);
    }

    public Task LogoutAsync()
    {
        _isAuthenticated = false;
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    public Task<bool> SendPasswordResetEmailAsync(string email)
    {
        return Task.FromResult(!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email));
    }

    public Task<bool> ResetPasswordAsync(string token, string newPassword)
    {
        return Task.FromResult(!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newPassword));
    }

    public Task<bool> IsAuthenticatedAsync()
    {
        return Task.FromResult(_isAuthenticated);
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Venther.App.Models;
using Venther.App.Services.Interfaces;
using Device = Venther.App.Models.Device;

namespace Venther.App.Services.Mocks;

public class MockDeviceService : IDeviceService
{
    private readonly List<Device> _devices = new()
    {
        new Device
        {
            Id = "DEV-001",
            Name = "Chiller Principal - Ala Sul",
            Location = "Ed. Corporate Tower - 4Âº Andar",
            Status = DeviceStatus.Normal,
            LastReading = "7.2Â°C â€¢ OperaÃ§Ã£o normal"
        },
        new Device
        {
            Id = "DEV-002",
            Name = "Fancoil AuditÃ³rio Principal",
            Location = "TÃ©rreo",
            Status = DeviceStatus.Normal,
            LastReading = "21.0Â°C â€¢ EstÃ¡vel"
        },
        new Device
        {
            Id = "DEV-003",
            Name = "Self Contained - Sala de Servidores",
            Location = "Subsolo",
            Status = DeviceStatus.Attention,
            LastReading = "Filtro saturado detectado"
        },
        new Device
        {
            Id = "DEV-004",
            Name = "VRF Condensadora 02",
            Location = "Cobertura - Bloco B",
            Status = DeviceStatus.Critical,
            LastReading = "PressÃ£o alta de descarga"
        }
    };

    public Task<IReadOnlyList<Device>> GetDevicesAsync()
    {
        return Task.FromResult<IReadOnlyList<Device>>(_devices.AsReadOnly());
    }

    public Task<Device?> GetDeviceByIdAsync(string id)
    {
        var device = _devices.FirstOrDefault(d => string.Equals(d.Id, id, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        return Task.FromResult(device);
    }

    public Task<bool> RegisterDeviceAsync(Device device)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(device.Id))
        {
            device.Id = $"DEV-{_devices.Count + 1:D3}";
        }
        _devices.Add(device);
        return Task.FromResult(true);
    }

    public Task<bool> UpdateDeviceStatusAsync(string id, DeviceStatus status)
    {
        var device = _devices.FirstOrDefault(d => string.Equals(d.Id, id, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        if (device == null)
            return Task.FromResult(false);

        device.Status = status;
        return Task.FromResult(true);
    }

    public Task<DeviceTelemetry> GetTelemetryAsync(string deviceId) =>
    Task.FromResult(new DeviceTelemetry(
        Supply: new(12.4, "EstÃ¡vel", false),
        Return: new(22.8, "Ideal", false),
        GasPressure: new(4.2, "Normal", false),
        EvaporatorDeltaP: new(180, "Limpo", false),
        OperationalPercent: 100));

    public Task<IReadOnlyList<double>> GetTemperatureTrendAsync(string deviceId, TrendPeriod period)
    {
        double[] data = period switch
        {
            TrendPeriod.OneHour => [12.9, 12.7, 12.6, 12.8, 13.1, 13.3, 13.2, 12.9, 12.5, 12.4, 12.6, 12.9, 12.4],
            TrendPeriod.TwelveHours => [13.5, 13.2, 12.8, 12.6, 12.9, 13.4, 13.8, 13.5, 13.0, 12.7, 12.5, 12.4],
            _ => [14.0, 13.6, 13.1, 12.8, 12.5, 12.7, 13.2, 13.9, 14.1, 13.6, 13.0, 12.4]
        };
        return Task.FromResult<IReadOnlyList<double>>(data);
    }
}

    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(workOrder.Id))
            workOrder.Id = $"OS-{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd-HHmm}";
        _workOrders.Add(workOrder);
        return Task.FromResult(true);

    public Task<bool> CompleteWorkOrderAsync(string id)
    {
        var item = _workOrders.FirstOrDefault(w => string.Equals(w.Id, id, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        if (item == null) return Task.FromResult(false);
        item.IsDone = true;
        item.Status = WorkOrderStatus.Done;
        return Task.FromResult(true);
    }

--- FILE: ViewModels/AlertsViewModel.cs ---
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Venther.App.Controls;
using Venther.App.Helpers;
using Venther.App.Models;
using Venther.App.Services.Interfaces;

namespace Venther.App.ViewModels;

public partial class AlertsViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    private readonly IAlertService _alertService;
    private System.Collections.Generic.List<AlertItem> _allAlerts = new();

    [ObservableProperty]
    private ObservableCollection<AlertItem> _filteredAlerts = new();

    [ObservableProperty]
    private ObservableCollection<SegmentItem> _filterSegments = new();

    [ObservableProperty]
    private int _selectedSegmentIndex;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private int _activeAlertsCount;

    // TODO: No .NET MAUI Shell padrÃ£o nÃ£o hÃ¡ propriedade nativa Tab.BadgeCount multiplataforma.
    // Para exibir o badge na TabBar no Android, implementar um ShellBottomNavViewAppearanceTracker
    // customizado utilizando bottomNavigationView.GetOrCreateBadge(tabIndex).Number = ActiveAlertsCount.

    public AlertsViewModel(IAlertService alertService)
    {
        _alertService = alertService;
        Title = "Alertas";
        InitSegments();
    }

    private void InitSegments()
    {
        FilterSegments = new ObservableCollection<SegmentItem>
        {
            new SegmentItem("Todos",    0, "all"),
            new SegmentItem("CrÃ­ticos", 0, "critical"),
            new SegmentItem("Avisos",   0, "warning"),
            new SegmentItem("Resolvidos", 0, "resolved"),
        };
    }

    partial void OnSelectedSegmentIndexChanged(int value)
    {
        ApplyFilter();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task Refresh()
    {
        if (IsBusy)
            return;

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            var alerts = await _alertService.GetAlertsAsync();
            _allAlerts = alerts.ToList();

            // Update segment counts
            int totalCount    = _allAlerts.Count;
            int criticalCount = _allAlerts.Count(a => !a.IsResolved && a.Severity == AlertSeverity.Critical);
            int warningCount  = _allAlerts.Count(a => !a.IsResolved && a.Severity == AlertSeverity.Warning);
            int resolvedCount = _allAlerts.Count(a => a.IsResolved);

            FilterSegments[0].Count = totalCount;
            FilterSegments[1].Count = criticalCount;
            FilterSegments[2].Count = warningCount;
            FilterSegments[3].Count = resolvedCount;

            ActiveAlertsCount = criticalCount + warningCount;

            ApplyFilter();
        }
        finally
        {
            IsBusy = false;
        }
    }

    private void ApplyFilter()
    {
        var key = SelectedSegmentIndex >= 0 && SelectedSegmentIndex < FilterSegments.Count
            ? FilterSegments[SelectedSegmentIndex].Key
            : "all";

        var filtered = key switch
        {
            "critical" => _allAlerts.Where(a => !a.IsResolved && a.Severity == AlertSeverity.Critical),
            "warning"  => _allAlerts.Where(a => !a.IsResolved && a.Severity == AlertSeverity.Warning),
            "resolved" => _allAlerts.Where(a => a.IsResolved),
            _          => _allAlerts.AsEnumerable(),
        };

        FilteredAlerts = new ObservableCollection<AlertItem>(filtered);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task ViewDetail(AlertItem? alert)
    {
        if (alert == null)
            return;

        await Shell.Current.GoToAsync(Routes.DeviceDetail, new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, object>
        {
            ["Alert"] = alert,
            ["AlertId"] = alert.Id
        });
    }
}

namespace Venther.App.ViewModels;
