--- FILE: ViewModels/DeviceDetailViewModel.cs ---
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using System.Globalization;
using Venther.App.Models;
using Venther.App.Services.Interfaces;
using static Android.Telephony.CarrierConfigManager;

namespace Venther.App.ViewModels;

public record MetricViewData(string Value, string Unit, string Caption, bool IsAlert)
{
    public static MetricViewData Empty => new("--", "", "", false);
}

[QueryProperty(nameof(DeviceId), "id")]
public partial class DeviceDetailViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    private static readonly CultureInfo Inv = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture; // Figma usa ponto (12.4)
    private readonly IDeviceService _deviceService;

    public DeviceDetailViewModel(IDeviceService deviceService)
    {
        _deviceService = deviceService;
        Title = "Detalhes do Equipamento";
    }

    [ObservableProperty] private string? deviceId;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool isRealtimeTab = true;
    [ObservableProperty] private TrendPeriod selectedPeriod = TrendPeriod.OneHour;
    [ObservableProperty] private IReadOnlyList<double> trendValues = Array.Empty<double>();

    [ObservableProperty] private MetricViewData supply = MetricViewData.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private MetricViewData returnTemp = MetricViewData.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private MetricViewData gas = MetricViewData.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private MetricViewData deltaP = MetricViewData.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty] private string statusLabel = "Normal (100%)";
    [ObservableProperty] private bool hasAlert;
    [ObservableProperty] private string nowLabel = "Agora: --";

    public bool IsHistoryTab => !IsRealtimeTab;
    public bool IsPeriod1h => SelectedPeriod == TrendPeriod.OneHour;
    public bool IsPeriod12h => SelectedPeriod == TrendPeriod.TwelveHours;
    public bool IsPeriod24h => SelectedPeriod == TrendPeriod.TwentyFourHours;
    public string TrendStartLabel => SelectedPeriod switch
    {
        TrendPeriod.OneHour => "HÃ¡ 60 minutos",
        TrendPeriod.TwelveHours => "HÃ¡ 12 horas",
        _ => "HÃ¡ 24 horas"
    };

    partial void OnIsRealtimeTabChanged(bool value) => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsHistoryTab));

    partial void OnSelectedPeriodChanged(TrendPeriod value)
    {
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsPeriod1h));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsPeriod12h));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsPeriod24h));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TrendStartLabel));
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        if (IsBusy) return;
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            var id = DeviceId ?? "mock";
            var t = await _deviceService.GetTelemetryAsync(id);

            Supply = new($"{t.Supply.Value.ToString("0.0", Inv)}Â°C", "", t.Supply.StatusText, t.Supply.IsAlert);
            ReturnTemp = new($"{t.Return.Value.ToString("0.0", Inv)}Â°C", "", t.Return.StatusText, t.Return.IsAlert);
            Gas = new(t.GasPressure.Value.ToString("0.0", Inv), "bar", t.GasPressure.StatusText, t.GasPressure.IsAlert);
            DeltaP = new(t.EvaporatorDeltaP.Value.ToString("0", Inv), "Pa", t.EvaporatorDeltaP.StatusText, t.EvaporatorDeltaP.IsAlert);

            HasAlert = Supply.IsAlert || ReturnTemp.IsAlert || Gas.IsAlert || DeltaP.IsAlert;
            StatusLabel = $"{(HasAlert ? "AtenÃ§Ã£o" : "Normal")} ({t.OperationalPercent}%)";
            NowLabel = $"Agora: {t.Supply.Value.ToString("0.0", Inv)}Â°C";

            TrendValues = await _deviceService.GetTemperatureTrendAsync(id, SelectedPeriod);
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void SelectTab(string tab) => IsRealtimeTab = tab == "realtime";

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SelectPeriodAsync(string period)
    {
        SelectedPeriod = period switch
        {
            "12h" => TrendPeriod.TwelveHours,
            "24h" => TrendPeriod.TwentyFourHours,
            _ => TrendPeriod.OneHour
        };
        TrendValues = await _deviceService.GetTemperatureTrendAsync(DeviceId ?? "mock", SelectedPeriod);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private Task BackAsync() => Shell.Current.GoToAsync("..");

    [RelayCommand]
    private Task RequestMaintenanceAsync() =>
        Shell.Current.DisplayAlert("ManutenÃ§Ã£o Preventiva", "SolicitaÃ§Ã£o enviada (mock).", "OK"); // TODO: API

    [RelayCommand]
    private Task DownloadReportAsync() =>
        Shell.Current.DisplayAlert("RelatÃ³rio PMOC", "Download disponÃ­vel quando a API estiver pronta.", "OK"); // TODO: API
}

--- FILE: ViewModels/HomeViewModel.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Venther.App.Helpers;
using Venther.App.Models;
using Venther.App.Services.Interfaces;
using Device = Venther.App.Models.Device;

namespace Venther.App.ViewModels;

public partial class HomeViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    private readonly IDeviceService _deviceService;
    private List<Device> _allDevices = new();

    [ObservableProperty]
    private string _searchText = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private int _totalCount;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private int _normalCount;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private int _attentionCount;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private string _activeFilter = "Total";

    public ObservableCollection<Device> FilteredDevices { get; } = new();

    public HomeViewModel(IDeviceService deviceService)
    {
        _deviceService = deviceService;
        Title = "InÃ­cio";
        _ = LoadDevicesAsync();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    public async Task LoadDevicesAsync()
    {
        if (IsBusy)
            return;

        try
        {
            IsBusy = true;
            var list = await _deviceService.GetDevicesAsync();
            _allDevices = list.ToList();

            UpdateCounts();
            ApplyFilters();
        }
        finally
        {
            IsBusy = false;
        }
    }

    partial void OnSearchTextChanged(string value)
    {
        ApplyFilters();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void SelectFilter(string filter)
    {
        ActiveFilter = filter;
        ApplyFilters();
    }

    private void UpdateCounts()
    {
        TotalCount = _allDevices.Count;
        NormalCount = _allDevices.Count(d => d.Status == DeviceStatus.Normal);
        AttentionCount = _allDevices.Count(d => d.Status == DeviceStatus.Attention || d.Status == DeviceStatus.Critical);
    }

    private void ApplyFilters()
    {
        var filtered = _allDevices.AsEnumerable();

        if (ActiveFilter == "Normal")
        {
            filtered = filtered.Where(d => d.Status == DeviceStatus.Normal);
        }
        else if (ActiveFilter == "Attention")
        {
            filtered = filtered.Where(d => d.Status == DeviceStatus.Attention || d.Status == DeviceStatus.Critical);
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SearchText))
        {
            var term = SearchText.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
            filtered = filtered.Where(d =>
                (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(d.Name) && d.Name.ToLowerInvariant().Contains(term)) ||
                (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(d.Location) && d.Location.ToLowerInvariant().Contains(term)) ||
                (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(d.Id) && d.Id.ToLowerInvariant().Contains(term)));
        }

        FilteredDevices.Clear();
        foreach (var item in filtered)
        {
            FilteredDevices.Add(item);
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task DeviceTapped(Device? device)
    {
        if (device == null)
            return;

        var navParams = new Dictionary<string, object>
        {
            ["Device"] = device,
            ["DeviceId"] = device.Id
        };
        await Shell.Current.GoToAsync(Routes.DeviceDetail, navParams);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task RegisterDevice()
    {
        await Shell.Current.GoToAsync(Routes.RegisterDevice);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task OpenAlerts()
    {
        await Shell.Current.GoToAsync("//main/alerts");
    }
}
--- FILE: ViewModels/LoginViewModel.cs ---
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Venther.App.Helpers;
using Venther.App.Services.Interfaces;

namespace Venther.App.ViewModels;

public partial class LoginViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    private readonly IAuthService _authService;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private string _email = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private string _password = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private bool _hasEmailError;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private string _emailErrorMessage = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private bool _hasPasswordError;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private string _passwordErrorMessage = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private bool _hasCredentialError;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private string _credentialErrorMessage = string.Empty;

    public bool IsNotBusy => !IsBusy;

    public LoginViewModel(IAuthService authService)
    {
        _authService = authService;
        Title = "Login";
    }

    partial void OnEmailChanged(string value)
    {
        if (HasEmailError)
        {
            HasEmailError = false;
            EmailErrorMessage = string.Empty;
            if (!HasPasswordError)
            {
                HasCredentialError = false;
                CredentialErrorMessage = string.Empty;
            }
        }
    }

    partial void OnPasswordChanged(string value)
    {
        if (HasPasswordError)
        {
            HasPasswordError = false;
            PasswordErrorMessage = string.Empty;
            if (!HasEmailError)
            {
                HasCredentialError = false;
                CredentialErrorMessage = string.Empty;
            }
        }
    }

    protected override void OnPropertyChanged(System.ComponentModel.PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
        if (e.PropertyName == nameof(IsBusy))
        {
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsNotBusy));
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task Entrar()
    {
        if (IsBusy)
            return;

        // Limpa estados de erro anteriores
        HasEmailError = false;
        EmailErrorMessage = string.Empty;
        HasPasswordError = false;
        PasswordErrorMessage = string.Empty;
        HasCredentialError = false;
        CredentialErrorMessage = string.Empty;

        bool isValid = true;

        // ValidaÃ§Ã£o de E-mail via Validators
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Email))
        {
            HasEmailError = true;
            EmailErrorMessage = "Informe seu e-mail.";
            isValid = false;
        }
        else if (!Validators.IsValidEmail(Email))
        {
            HasEmailError = true;
            EmailErrorMessage = "E-mail invÃ¡lido. Utilize o formato nome@empresa.com.";
            isValid = false;
        }

        // ValidaÃ§Ã£o de Senha via Validators
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Password))
        {
            HasPasswordError = true;
            PasswordErrorMessage = "Informe sua senha.";
            isValid = false;
        }
        else if (!Validators.IsValidPassword(Password))
        {
            HasPasswordError = true;
            PasswordErrorMessage = "MÃ­nimo 8 caracteres, 1 maiÃºscula e 1 caractere especial.";
            isValid = false;
        }

        if (!isValid)
        {
            HasCredentialError = true;
            CredentialErrorMessage = "Credenciais invÃ¡lidas. Verifique os campos destacados.";
            return;
        }

        try
        {
            IsBusy = true;

            var success = await _authService.LoginAsync(Email, Password);

            if (success)
            {
                await Shell.Current.GoToAsync("//main/home");
            }
            else
            {
                HasCredentialError = true;
                CredentialErrorMessage = "E-mail ou senha incorretos. Verifique suas credenciais.";
                HasEmailError = true;
                HasPasswordError = true;
            }
        }
        catch (Exception)
        {
            HasCredentialError = true;
            CredentialErrorMessage = "Erro de conexÃ£o ao autenticar. Tente novamente.";
        }
        finally
        {
            IsBusy = false;
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task ForgotPassword()
    {
        await Shell.Current.GoToAsync(Routes.ForgotPassword);
    }
}

--- FILE: ViewModels/WorkOrdersViewModel.cs ---
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Venther.App.Controls;
using Venther.App.Helpers;
using Venther.App.Models;
using Venther.App.Services.Interfaces;

namespace Venther.App.ViewModels;

public partial class WorkOrdersViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    private readonly IWorkOrderService _workOrderService;
    private List<WorkOrder> _allWorkOrders = new();

    [ObservableProperty]
    private ObservableCollection<WorkOrder> _filteredWorkOrders = new();

    [ObservableProperty]
    private ObservableCollection<SegmentItem> _filterSegments = new();

    [ObservableProperty]
    private int _selectedSegmentIndex;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private int _activeCount;

    public WorkOrdersViewModel(IWorkOrderService workOrderService)
    {
        _workOrderService = workOrderService;
        Title = "Chamados";
        InitSegments();
    }

    private void InitSegments()
    {
        FilterSegments = new ObservableCollection<SegmentItem>
        {
            new SegmentItem("Todas",      0, "all"),
            new SegmentItem("Em andamento", 0, "inprogress"),
            new SegmentItem("Agendadas",  0, "scheduled"),
            new SegmentItem("ConcluÃ­das", 0, "done"),
        };
    }

    partial void OnSelectedSegmentIndexChanged(int value) => ApplyFilter();

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task Refresh()
    {
        if (IsBusy) return;
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            var orders = await _workOrderService.GetWorkOrdersAsync();
            _allWorkOrders = orders.ToList();

            FilterSegments[0].Count = _allWorkOrders.Count;
            FilterSegments[1].Count = _allWorkOrders.Count(w => w.Status == WorkOrderStatus.InProgress);
            FilterSegments[2].Count = _allWorkOrders.Count(w => w.Status == WorkOrderStatus.Scheduled);
            FilterSegments[3].Count = _allWorkOrders.Count(w => w.Status == WorkOrderStatus.Done);

            ActiveCount = FilterSegments[1].Count + FilterSegments[2].Count;

            ApplyFilter();
        }
        finally
        {
            IsBusy = false;
        }
    }

    private void ApplyFilter()
    {
        var key = SelectedSegmentIndex >= 0 && SelectedSegmentIndex < FilterSegments.Count
            ? FilterSegments[SelectedSegmentIndex].Key
            : "all";

        var filtered = key switch
        {
            "inprogress" => _allWorkOrders.Where(w => w.Status == WorkOrderStatus.InProgress),
            "scheduled"  => _allWorkOrders.Where(w => w.Status == WorkOrderStatus.Scheduled),
            "done"       => _allWorkOrders.Where(w => w.Status == WorkOrderStatus.Done),
            _            => _allWorkOrders.AsEnumerable(),
        };

        FilteredWorkOrders = new ObservableCollection<WorkOrder>(filtered);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task ViewDetail(WorkOrder? workOrder)
    {
        if (workOrder == null) return;

        await Shell.Current.GoToAsync(Routes.WorkOrderDetail, new Dictionary<string, object>
        {
            ["WorkOrderId"] = workOrder.Id,
            ["WorkOrder"] = workOrder
        });
    }

    // TODO: BotÃ£o de filtro avanÃ§ado â€” abre bottom-sheet com opÃ§Ãµes de ordenaÃ§Ã£o e filtro por perÃ­odo.
    [RelayCommand]
    private void OpenFilter()
    {
        // TODO: implementar filtro avanÃ§ado (bottom-sheet ou modal)
    }
}

--- FILE: Views/Devices/DeviceDetailPage.xaml.cs ---
using System.ComponentModel;
using Venther.App.Helpers;
using Venther.App.ViewModels;

namespace Venther.App.Views.Devices;

public partial class DeviceDetailPage : ContentPage
{
    private readonly DeviceDetailViewModel _vm;
    private readonly TrendChartDrawable _drawable = new();

    public DeviceDetailPage(DeviceDetailViewModel vm)
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        BindingContext = _vm = vm;
        TrendChart.Drawable = _drawable;
        _vm.PropertyChanged += OnVmPropertyChanged;
    }

    protected override async void OnAppearing()
    {
        base.OnAppearing();
        await _vm.LoadCommand.ExecuteAsync(null);
    }

    private void OnVmPropertyChanged(object? sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.PropertyName == nameof(DeviceDetailViewModel.TrendValues))
        {
            _drawable.Values = _vm.TrendValues;
            TrendChart.Invalidate();
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: Views/Home/HomePage.xaml.cs ---
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Venther.App.ViewModels;

namespace Venther.App.Views.Home;

public partial class HomePage : ContentPage
{
    public HomePage(HomeViewModel viewModel)
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        BindingContext = viewModel;
    }

    protected override void OnAppearing()
    {
        base.OnAppearing();
        if (BindingContext is HomeViewModel vm)
        {
            _ = vm.LoadDevicesAsync();
        }
    }
}

using Venther.App.ViewModels;

namespace Venther.App.Views.WorkOrders;

public partial class WorkOrdersPage : ContentPage
{
    private readonly WorkOrdersViewModel _vm;

    public WorkOrdersPage(WorkOrdersViewModel viewModel)
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        _vm = viewModel;
        BindingContext = viewModel;
    }

    protected override void OnAppearing()
    {
        base.OnAppearing();
        _vm.RefreshCommand.Execute(null);
    }
}
